## 10. Trondheim, Norway
* **Country / area:** Norway — Trøndelag.
* **Currency / language baseline:** NOK (cards accepted everywhere); Norwegian (English widely spoken).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Visit Trondheim (Official City Guide)](https://visittrondheim.no/en/) | [Port of Trondheim *(Trondheim Havn)*](https://trondheimhavn.no/en/)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships dock at **Pier 68 / Turistskipskaia** on Brattøra right beside the Rockheim museum and central railway station. Reaching Nidaros Cathedral and Bakklandet takes a flat 15–20 minute walk across the pedestrian footbridge over the rail tracks and along the Nidelva river. **Confirm per call:** pier berth at Turistskipskaia, Nidaros Cathedral visitor hours, and Trampe bicycle lift activity.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Nidaros Cathedral & The Archbishop's Palace *(Erkebispegården)*** | Walk-Off / Independent | **Travel:** 20–25 min flat walk from cruise pier.<br>**Physical:** Low (flat cathedral square and accessible nave; steep narrow stone spiral stairs if climbing central tower).<br>**Weather:** None (indoor cathedral and Crown Regalia museum).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | The world's northernmost medieval Gothic cathedral and Norway's national sanctuary, built over the burial site of Viking king St. Olav with a richly sculpted West Front. | [Nidaros Cathedral Official](https://www.nidarosdomen.no/en)<br>[Google Maps (63.4269, 10.3969)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=63.4269,10.3969) |
| **Old Town Bridge *(Gamle Bybro)* & Bakklandet Wooden Quarter** | Walk-Off / Independent | **Travel:** 15–20 min flat walk from cruise pier.<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (flat wooden bridge; cobblestone lanes in Bakklandet).<br>**Weather:** Low (cozy riverside cafes and artisan boutiques).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Cross the iconic red-timbered "Portal of Happiness" bridge to explore colourful 18th-century wooden wharves on stilts along the Nidelva River and the world's first bicycle lift (*Trampe*). | [Visit Trondheim — Bakklandet](https://visittrondheim.no/en/)<br>[Google Maps (63.4283, 10.4017)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=63.4283,10.4017) |
| **Kristiansten Fortress** | Walk-Off / Independent | **Travel:** 25 min walk from pier (including steep uphill climb from Bakklandet) or 10 min taxi.<br>**Physical:** Moderate-Strenuous (steep paved hill climb and grassy fortress ramparts).<br>**Weather:** Moderate (exposed hilltop viewpoint).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | A 17th-century hilltop fortress offering panoramic views over Trondheim's red rooftops, Nidaros Cathedral, and the Trondheimsfjord. | [Visit Trondheim — Kristiansten](https://visittrondheim.no/en/)<br>[Google Maps (63.4269, 10.4106)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=63.4269,10.4106) |
| **Ringve Music Museum & Munkholmen Island** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 15 min taxi/Bus 20 northeast to Ringve (or 15 min seasonal passenger boat from Ravnkloa to Munkholmen).<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (accessible botanical gardens/museum at Ringve; uneven island paths at Munkholmen).<br>**Weather:** Low at Ringve; Moderate at Munkholmen.<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes (choose one site). | Combines Norway's national museum of music and historic instruments set in botanical gardens with an atmospheric 11th-century island monastery and fortress in the fjord. | [Ringve Music Museum Official](https://ringve.no/en)<br>[Google Maps (63.4475, 10.4539)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=63.4475,10.4539) |

## 11. Visby, Gotland, Sweden
* **Country / area:** Sweden — Gotland Island *(Baltic Sea)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** SEK (cards universally accepted; cash rarely needed); Swedish (English widely spoken).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Gotland Official Visitor Guide *(Gotland.com)*](https://gotland.com/en/) | [Port of Visby *(Ports of Gotland)*](https://www.portsofgotland.com/)
* **Getting ashore:** Large cruise ships dock at the dedicated **Visby Cruise Quay** just south of the harbour, a flat 10–15 minute waterfront walk to the medieval city wall gates and Almedalen Park. Smaller vessels occasionally tender or berth inside the inner harbour right beside the town centre. Visby's Old Town rises on a steep limestone cliff, featuring hilly cobblestone streets. **Confirm per call:** Cruise Quay vs. inner harbour tender, sightseeing road-train schedule, and Gotland Museum hours.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Visby Medieval City Wall *(Ringmuren)*, Almedalen & Botanical Garden *(DBW)*** | Walk-Off / Independent | **Travel:** 10–15 min flat waterfront walk from Cruise Quay.<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (flat seaside park and botanical garden; cobbled lanes along the wall).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate (sheltered rose-lined lanes and historic gardens).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | A UNESCO World Heritage Hanseatic town encircled by a 3.4-kilometre 13th-century limestone ring wall with 44 watchtowers, lush rose-draped cottages, and sea-facing botanical gardens. | [Gotland.com — Visby](https://gotland.com/en/)<br>[Google Maps (57.6419, 18.2917)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=57.6419,18.2917) |
| **Gotland Museum *(Fornsalen)* & St. Karin Church Ruins** | Walk-Off / Independent | **Travel:** 15 min walk from Cruise Quay into central Stora Torget.<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (accessible museum galleries; uneven cobbles in Stora Torget).<br>**Weather:** None (indoor museum; open-air Gothic church arches in the main square).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Displays extraordinary Viking silver hoards, ancient Gotlandic picture stones, and medieval wooden sculptures steps from the dramatic vaulted ruins of St. Karin's church. | [Gotland Museum Official](https://www.gotlandsmuseum.se/en/)<br>[Google Maps (57.6394, 18.2925)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=57.6394,18.2925) |
| **Visby Cathedral *(Sankta Maria)* & Klinten Cliff Viewpoint** | Walk-Off / Independent | **Travel:** 20–25 min walk from pier (steep uphill cobbles and stairs up to Klinten).<br>**Physical:** Moderate-Strenuous (steep cobbled lanes and stone steps up the limestone cliff).<br>**Weather:** Moderate (exposed cliffside viewpoint).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Combines Gotland's sole surviving medieval cathedral with a hilltop neighbourhood offering sweeping views over Visby's terracotta roofs, church ruins, and the Baltic Sea. | [Gotland.com — Visby Cathedral](https://gotland.com/en/)<br>[Google Maps (57.6417, 18.2981)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=57.6417,18.2981) |
| **Lummelunda Cave *(Lummelundagrottan)* & Högklint Sea Cliffs** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 15–20 min drive north to Lummelunda (or 15 min south to Högklint).<br>**Physical:** Moderate (guided cave walkways with steps; rocky cliffside paths at Högklint).<br>**Weather:** Low (constant 8°C inside cave; exposed sea cliffs at Högklint).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes (2.5–3 hr tour). | Explores a 400-million-year-old karst limestone cavern filled with stalactites and subterranean lakes alongside dramatic 48-metre coastal cliffs overlooking Visby. | [Lummelundagrottan Official](https://lummelundagrottan.se/en/)<br>[Google Maps (57.7381, 18.4044)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=57.7381,18.4044) |

## 12. Warnemünde (Berlin), Germany
* **Country / area:** Germany — Mecklenburg-Vorpommern *(Baltic Coast; gateway to Rostock & Berlin)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** EUR; German (English widely spoken in tourist hubs).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Rostock & Warnemünde Official Tourism](https://www.rostock.de/en/) | [Rostock Port — Cruise Shipping](https://www.rostock-port.de/en/cruise-shipping.html)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships berth at **Warnemünde Cruise Center** (Piers P1–P8), right beside the Warnemünde S-Bahn train station, Alter Strom canal, and Baltic beach. Passengers step off the gangway directly into the charming seaside resort town. Reaching Berlin requires a 2.5–3 hour drive or train journey each way. **Confirm per call:** pier berth (P1–P8), S-Bahn timetable to Rostock, and Berlin excursion departure time.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Alter Strom Canal, Warnemünde Lighthouse & Teepott** | Walk-Off / Independent | **Travel:** 5–10 min flat walk directly from cruise terminal.<br>**Physical:** Low (flat harbourside promenade; narrow spiral stairs if climbing lighthouse).<br>**Weather:** Moderate (breezy Baltic beachfront; sheltered canal-side cafes and fish-roll stands).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Stroll past historic captains' houses and fishing cutters selling fresh *Fischbrötchen* along the Old Stream canal, then climb the 1898 lighthouse beside the modernist Teepott pavilion and wide sandy beach. | [Rostock & Warnemünde Tourism](https://www.rostock.de/en/)<br>[Google Maps (54.1819, 12.0861)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=54.1819,12.0861) |
| **Hanseatic City of Rostock *(St. Mary's Church & Kröpeliner Straße)*** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 20 min direct S-Bahn commuter train (S1/S2/S3) from Warnemünde station (right beside pier) + 10 min walk/tram.<br>**Physical:** Low (flat pedestrianized historic centre and cobbles).<br>**Weather:** Low (indoor Brick Gothic churches and covered shopping arcades).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Explore one of Germany's historic Hanseatic League ports, famed for its colorful gabled merchant houses, University Square, and St. Mary's Church's working 1472 astronomical clock. | [Rostock Tourism — Old Town](https://www.rostock.de/en/)<br>[Google Maps (54.0889, 12.1389)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=54.0889,12.1389) |
| **Molli Narrow-Gauge Steam Railway & Bad Doberan Minster** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 25–30 min drive west to Bad Doberan (or regional train via Rostock).<br>**Physical:** Low (seated vintage steam train ride and level church interior).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate (covered heritage rail carriages; indoor Gothic abbey church).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes (3–3.5 hr excursion). | Ride a historic 1886 steam locomotive chugging right down the middle of Bad Doberan's cobbled streets to the Baltic coast after touring a grand Cistercian Brick Gothic minster. | [Mecklenburgische Bäderbahn Molli](https://www.molli-bahn.de/en/)<br>[Google Maps (54.1069, 11.9061)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=54.1069,11.9061) |

# Cruise Port Passenger Guide — Japan & South Korea Destination Content Dataset

> **Purpose:** Expanded destination-page content for the 9 Japan and South Korea ports in the cruise passenger app. Each port includes structured attraction descriptions, value propositions, practical logistics, verified visitor/tourism links, and coordinate-anchored Google Maps links ready for dynamic berth routing.

## Dynamic Routing Integration Note

To calculate real-time routes from the ship's confirmed pier or tender landing, replace the query links below with the Google Maps Directions URL template once the `SailingPortCall` record supplies `{berth_lat}` and `{berth_lng}`:

```text
https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin={berth_lat},{berth_lng}&destination={dest_lat},{dest_lng}
```

---

## 1. Aomori, Japan
* **Country / area:** Japan — Aomori Prefecture *(Northern Honshu / Tohoku)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** JPY (cash recommended for local markets/buses; IC cards and credit cards accepted at major venues); Japanese (limited English outside tourist hubs).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Amazing Aomori (Official Prefectural Guide)](https://amazing-aomori.jp/en/) | [Aomori City Tourism](https://www.atca.info/en/) | [Port of Aomori — Cruise Information](https://www.pref.aomori.lg.jp/soshiki/kendo/kowan/)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships dock at **Shin-Chuo Wharf** (adjacent to the A-Factory waterfront complex and JR Aomori Station, a 5–10 minute flat walk) or across the bay at **Okidate Pier** (25–30 minute walk or 10-minute shuttle/taxi to central Aomori). **Confirm per call:** Shin-Chuo vs. Okidate berth, port shuttle drop-off, and Oirase/Hirosaki seasonal bus schedules.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Nebuta Museum WA RASSE, A-Factory & Furukawa Fish Market *(Nokkedon)*** | Walk-Off / Independent | **Travel:** 5–10 min flat walk from Shin-Chuo Wharf (10 min shuttle/taxi from Okidate).<br>**Physical:** Low (fully accessible modern museum, waterfront market, and flat streets).<br>**Weather:** None (indoor museum, cidery hall, and covered fish market).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Displays towering illuminated warrior floats from Aomori's famous Nebuta Festival alongside a cider tasting hall and a DIY sashimi rice-bowl market. | [Nebuta Museum WA RASSE Official](https://www.nebuta.jp/warasse/foreign/english.html)<br>[Google Maps (40.8297, 140.7358)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=40.8297,140.7358) |
| **Sannai-Maruyama Jomon Site *(UNESCO World Heritage)*** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 20–25 min taxi or Nebutan-go shuttle bus from Aomori Station.<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (wide flat gravel/paved archaeological park paths and indoor museum).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate (indoor museum; open-air reconstructed thatched dwellings).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Japan's largest and best-preserved Jomon-period settlement (c. 3900–2200 BC), featuring massive chestnut-pillar towers, pit dwellings, and ancient pottery exhibits. | [Sannai-Maruyama Site Official](https://sannaimaruyama.pref.aomori.jp/english/)<br>[Google Maps (40.8114, 140.6969)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=40.8114,140.6969) |
| **Hirosaki Castle & Samurai District** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 40–45 min JR Ou Line train from Aomori Station + 15 min local bus/taxi (or 50 min direct drive).<br>**Physical:** Moderate (expansive castle park paths, wooden bridge, steep internal castle stairs).<br>**Weather:** Moderate (open-air parklands; renowned for spring cherry blossoms and autumn foliage).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Borderline (allow 4.5–5 hours round-trip). | Features one of Japan's twelve surviving original Edo-period castle keeps surrounded by triple moats, historic samurai residences, and apple orchards. | [Hirosaki Tourism Official](https://www.hirosaki-kanko.or.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (40.6075, 140.4642)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=40.6075,140.4642) |
| **Oirase Gorge & Lake Towada** | Full-Day / Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 75–90 min scenic mountain drive south each way.<br>**Physical:** Moderate (paved roadside viewpoints or optional riverside forest boardwalks).<br>**Weather:** Moderate-High (mountain valley weather; cool/rainy conditions possible).<br>**4-Hr Call:** No (strictly a 6.5–8 hour full-day excursion). | Follows a crystal-clear mountain stream rushing past moss-covered boulders and cascading waterfalls into a dramatic double-caldera volcanic lake. | [Amazing Aomori — Oirase Gorge](https://amazing-aomori.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (40.5389, 140.9469)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=40.5389,140.9469) |

## 2. Busan, South Korea
* **Country / area:** South Korea — Yeongnam *(Southeastern Coast)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** KRW (credit cards widely accepted; T-money card/cash useful for transit and street markets); Korean (English signage on metro/major sights).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Visit Busan (Official City Guide)](https://www.visitbusan.net/en/index.do) | [Busan Port Authority — Cruise Terminal](https://www.busanpa.com/eng/)
* **Getting ashore:** Most large cruise ships dock at the **Busan International Cruise Terminal** on Yeongdo Island (15–20 minute taxi or cruise shuttle across the bridge to Nampo-dong/Jagalchi) or at the **Busan Port International Passenger Terminal** in Jung-gu (10-minute walk to Busan Station/KTX and Metro Line 1). **Confirm per call:** Yeongdo vs. Jung-gu terminal, shuttle drop-off at Lotte Department Store/Nampo-dong, and Haedong Yonggungsa traffic times.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Jagalchi Fish Market, BIFF Square & Yongdusan Park *(Busan Tower)*** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 15–20 min taxi/shuttle from Yeongdo terminal (or 10 min Metro Line 1 from Busan Station).<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (flat bustling market lanes; escalators up to Yongdusan Park).<br>**Weather:** Low (indoor seafood halls and covered market arcades).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | South Korea's largest seafood market, teeming with live tanks and upstairs grilling restaurants, paired with lively street-food alleys and panoramic harbour views from Busan Tower. | [Visit Busan — Jagalchi Market](https://www.visitbusan.net/en/index.do)<br>[Google Maps (35.0966, 129.0306)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.0966,129.0306) |
| **Gamcheon Culture Village** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 20–25 min taxi from cruise terminals (or Metro to Toseong + local green minibus).<br>**Physical:** Strenuous (very steep hillside alleys, narrow stairways, and uneven slopes).<br>**Weather:** Moderate (exposed hillside neighbourhood).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Known as the "Machu Picchu of Busan," this hillside labyrinth of pastel-painted houses, murals, and artisan cafes overlooks Busan's working harbour. | [Visit Busan — Gamcheon Village](https://www.visitbusan.net/en/index.do)<br>[Google Maps (35.0975, 129.0106)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.0975,129.0106) |
| **Haedong Yonggungsa Seaside Temple & Haeundae Beach** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 45–60 min drive northeast via Gwangan Bridge (allow buffer for heavy coastal traffic).<br>**Physical:** Moderate (108 stone steps down to the coastal temple; flat beachfront promenade at Haeundae).<br>**Weather:** Moderate-High (exposed rocky ocean cliffs).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Borderline (allow 4.5–5 hours due to cross-city traffic). | A rare 14th-century Buddhist temple perched directly on dramatic seaside rocks above crashing waves, combined with Busan's famous crescent sand beach. | [Haedong Yonggungsa Official](http://www.yongkungsa.or.kr/en/)<br>[Google Maps (35.1884, 129.2233)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.1884,129.2233) |
| **Beomeosa Mountain Temple & UN Memorial Cemetery** | Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 30–45 min drive north/east depending on site.<br>**Physical:** Moderate (stone temple courtyards and steps; flat manicured memorial parklands).<br>**Weather:** Moderate.<br>**4-Hr Call:** No (allow 5–6 hours to combine both). | Contrasts a tranquil 1,300-year-old forested Silla-dynasty temple complex on Mount Geumjeongsan with the world's only UN-designated cemetery honouring Korean War soldiers. | [Visit Busan — Beomeosa](https://www.visitbusan.net/en/index.do)<br>[Google Maps (35.2836, 129.0906)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.2836,129.0906) |

## 3. Hakodate, Japan
* **Country / area:** Japan — Hokkaido *(Southern Hokkaido)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** JPY (cash recommended for morning market stalls and trams; cards accepted at major shops/restaurants); Japanese (basic English at tourist sites).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Travel Hakodate (Official City Guide)](https://www.hakodate.travel/en/) | [Port of Hakodate — Cruise Ships](https://www.city.hakodate.hokkaido.jp/docs/2014012000370/)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships berth at **Wakamatsu Pier** (right beside JR Hakodate Station and the Hakodate Morning Market, a 5-minute flat walk) or **Minatomachi Pier** (roughly 3 km north; 10-minute shuttle bus or taxi to downtown). **Confirm per call:** Wakamatsu vs. Minatomachi berth, Mount Hakodate ropeway wind/fog status, and streetcar day-pass booth.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Hakodate Morning Market *(Asaichi)* & Kanemori Red Brick Warehouses** | Walk-Off / Independent *(from Wakamatsu)* or Local Transit *(from Minatomachi)* | **Travel:** 5–15 min flat walk from Wakamatsu Pier (or 10 min shuttle/taxi from Minatomachi).<br>**Physical:** Low (flat waterfront promenades and covered market aisles).<br>**Weather:** Low (indoor seafood stalls and converted brick shopping halls).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Sample ultra-fresh Hokkaido uni (sea urchin), king crab, and squid at over 250 market stalls before browsing historic 19th-century red-brick waterfront warehouses. | [Travel Hakodate — Bay Area](https://www.hakodate.travel/en/)<br>[Google Maps (41.7661, 140.7167)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=41.7661,140.7167) |
| **Motomachi Historic Slope District & Mount Hakodate Ropeway** | Walk-Off / Independent or Local Tram | **Travel:** 20–25 min walk (or 10 min city tram to Jujigai) + steep slope walk + 3 min aerial ropeway.<br>**Physical:** Moderate-Strenuous (steep paved historic hills in Motomachi; step-free ropeway gondola and summit terraces).<br>**Weather:** High (334m summit often enveloped in coastal fog/wind; check live summit camera).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Explores 19th-century Russian Orthodox, Catholic, and Western consular architecture along steep harbour-view slopes, crowned by a cable car ride to sweeping views over Hakodate's hourglass peninsula. | [Mount Hakodate Ropeway Official](https://334.co.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (41.7594, 140.7042)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=41.7594,140.7042) |
| **Fort Goryōkaku & Goryōkaku Tower** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 15–20 min city tram (to Goryokaku-Koen-Mae) + 10 min flat walk (or 15 min taxi from port).<br>**Physical:** Low (high-speed elevator to tower deck; flat moat-side park paths).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate (enclosed glass observation tower; open-air star-fortress park).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Japan's first Western-style star-shaped citadel (1864), best admired from the 107-metre Goryōkaku Tower overlooking its five-pointed moats and reconstructed magistrate's office. | [Goryōkaku Tower Official](https://www.goryokaku-tower.co.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (41.7969, 140.7569)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=41.7969,140.7569) |
| **Onuma Quasi-National Park** | Full-Day / Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 45–50 min drive north (or 30 min JR Limited Express train to Onuma-Koen Station).<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (flat lakeside walking paths and arched island bridges).<br>**Weather:** Moderate (open volcanic lake plateau facing Mount Komagatake).<br>**4-Hr Call:** No (allow 4.5–5.5 hours). | A scenic volcanic lake landscape dotted with 126 wooded islets connected by picturesque footbridges beneath dormant Mount Komagatake. | [Travel Hakodate — Onuma Park](https://www.hakodate.travel/en/)<br>[Google Maps (41.9819, 140.6789)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=41.9819,140.6789) |

## 4. Hiroshima, Japan
* **Country / area:** Japan — Hiroshima Prefecture *(Seto Inland Sea)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** JPY (cards/IC cards widely accepted; cash helpful for small okonomiyaki shops); Japanese (good English signage at Peace Park and Miyajima).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Dive! Hiroshima (Official City & Prefectural Guide)](https://dive-hiroshima.com/en/) | [Port of Hiroshima — Cruise Information](https://www.pref.hiroshima.lg.jp/site/minato/)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships berth at **Itsukaichi Pier** (roughly 10 km west of downtown; 25–30 minute cruise shuttle, taxi, or Hiroden streetcar Line 2) or **Ujina Pier** (south of downtown; 20-minute Hiroden streetcar Line 1/5 or 15-minute taxi to Peace Park, with direct high-speed ferries to Miyajima right next door). **Confirm per call:** Itsukaichi vs. Ujina berth, Miyajima high-speed boat boarding, and shuttle drop-off point.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Hiroshima Peace Memorial Park, A-Bomb Dome & Museum** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 20–30 min Hiroden streetcar or taxi from either cruise pier.<br>**Physical:** Low (flat riverside memorial park and accessible museum galleries).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate (outdoor monuments; indoor museum).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | A deeply moving UNESCO World Heritage site preserving the skeletal Atomic Bomb Dome, Children's Peace Monument, and poignant historical exhibits dedicated to nuclear disarmament. | [Hiroshima Peace Memorial Museum Official](https://hpmmuseum.jp/?lang=eng)<br>[Google Maps (34.3955, 132.4536)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=34.3955,132.4536) |
| **Miyajima Island *(Itsukushima Shrine & Floating Torii Gate)*** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 25 min high-speed boat directly from Ujina Port (or 30 min train/tram to Miyajimaguchi + 10 min JR ferry).<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (flat seaside promenades, wooden shrine corridors, tame roaming deer).<br>**Weather:** Moderate (exposed coastal island; check tide tables for floating vs. walkable torii gate).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Borderline (allow 4.5–5 hours round-trip). | One of Japan's three most celebrated scenic views, famed for its vermilion 12th-century shrine and giant *torii* gate appearing to float on the Seto Inland Sea at high tide. | [Visit Miyajima Official](https://www.miyajima.or.jp/english/)<br>[Google Maps (34.2961, 132.3198)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=34.2961,132.3198) |
| **Hiroshima Castle & Shukkeien Garden** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 15 min walk northeast from Peace Memorial Park (or 20 min taxi from port).<br>**Physical:** Moderate (5-storey castle keep has steep interior stairs; Shukkeien has arched stone bridges and gravel paths).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate.<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Pairs a reconstructed 16th-century samurai carp castle with an Edo-period miniature landscape garden featuring koi ponds, tea houses, and sculpted valleys. | [Dive! Hiroshima — Shukkeien](https://dive-hiroshima.com/en/)<br>[Google Maps (34.4025, 132.4594)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=34.4025,132.4594) |
| **Okonomimura *(Hiroshima Okonomiyaki Village)*** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 10 min walk east from Peace Park in central Hatchobori/Shintenchi.<br>**Physical:** Low (elevator-accessible multi-floor dining building).<br>**Weather:** None (indoor teppanyaki counters).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Three floors of sizzling iron-griddle stalls serving Hiroshima's signature layered savory pancake loaded with cabbage, noodles, pork/seafood, and sweet-savory sauce. | [Okonomimura Official](http://www.okonomimura.jp/foreign/english.html)<br>[Google Maps (34.3925, 132.4619)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=34.3925,132.4619) |

## 5. Jeju, South Korea
* **Country / area:** South Korea — Jeju Special Self-Governing Province *(Jeju Island)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** KRW (cards widely accepted; cash helpful at markets); Korean (English signage at major UNESCO attractions; hiring a vetted taxi/tour is recommended due to island distances).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Visit Jeju (Official Provincial Guide)](https://www.visitjeju.net/en) | [Jeju Cruise Port Information](https://www.jeju.go.kr/)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships berth either in the north at **Jeju Port International Passenger Terminal** (Jeju City, 10 minutes from Dongmun Market) or in the south at **Seogwipo Gangjeong Cruise Terminal** (near Jungmun Resort and Cheonjiyeon Falls). Because Jeju is a large volcanic island, verifying which of the two ports your ship uses is essential before planning routes. **Confirm per call:** Jeju City vs. Seogwipo Gangjeong berth, metered taxi/charter rates, and Seongsan Ilchulbong travel time.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Seongsan Ilchulbong *(Sunrise Peak — UNESCO World Heritage)*** | Guided Excursion or Private Taxi | **Travel:** 55–65 min drive east from either Jeju City or Seogwipo cruise ports.<br>**Physical:** Strenuous to crater rim (steep 25–30 min stair climb); Low-Moderate along free coastal trail to *haenyeo* women diver cove.<br>**Weather:** High (exposed volcanic tuff cone; strong coastal winds).<br>**4-Hr Call:** No (allow 4.5–5.5 hours round-trip). | A dramatic 5,000-year-old volcanic crater rising straight out of the ocean, offering sweeping coastal panoramas and live demonstrations by Jeju's famed *haenyeo* free-diving women. | [Visit Jeju — Seongsan Ilchulbong](https://www.visitjeju.net/en)<br>[Google Maps (33.4581, 126.9425)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=33.4581,126.9425) |
| **Cheonjiyeon Waterfall & Jusangjeolli Volcanic Cliffs *(South Coast / Seogwipo)*** | Local Transit / Independent *(from Seogwipo)* or Guided Excursion *(from Jeju City)* | **Travel:** 15–20 min taxi from Seogwipo Gangjeong port (or 50–60 min drive south from Jeju City port).<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (paved riverside path to Cheonjiyeon; wooden viewing decks at Jusangjeolli).<br>**Weather:** Moderate.<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes (from Seogwipo); No (from Jeju City). | Combines a lush subtropical gorge waterfall with striking hexagonal black basalt rock pillars carved by cooling lava meeting crashing waves. | [Visit Jeju — Jusangjeolli](https://www.visitjeju.net/en)<br>[Google Maps (33.2378, 126.4250)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=33.2378,126.4250) |
| **Dongmun Traditional Market & Yongduam *(Dragon Head Rock) (North Coast / Jeju City)*** | Local Transit / Independent *(from Jeju City)* or Guided Excursion *(from Seogwipo)* | **Travel:** 10 min taxi from Jeju City cruise terminal (or 50–60 min drive north from Seogwipo port).<br>**Physical:** Low (flat covered market alleys; some coastal rock steps at Yongduam).<br>**Weather:** Low (covered market arcades).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes (from Jeju City); No (from Seogwipo). | Jeju's oldest bustling street market, packed with Hallabong tangerines, black pork skewers, abalone, and freshly baked tangerine pastries beside volcanic coastal rock formations. | [Visit Jeju — Dongmun Market](https://www.visitjeju.net/en)<br>[Google Maps (33.5119, 126.5281)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=33.5119,126.5281) |
| **Manjanggul Lava Tube & Hallasan National Park *(Eoseungsaengak Trail)*** | Full-Day / Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 45–60 min drive inland/northeast depending on berth.<br>**Physical:** Moderate-Strenuous (damp, uneven volcanic cave floor or stepped mountain trail).<br>**Weather:** Low inside lava tube (constant 11–15°C); High on Hallasan slopes.<br>**4-Hr Call:** No (allow 5–6 hours; verify Manjanggul maintenance status before visiting). | Explores Jeju's UNESCO-listed volcanic geology, including one of the world's finest lava tunnels and forested trails on South Korea's highest mountain. | [Visit Jeju — Hallasan](https://www.visitjeju.net/en)<br>[Google Maps (33.3617, 126.5292)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=33.3617,126.5292) |

## 6. Kagoshima, Japan
* **Country / area:** Japan — Kagoshima Prefecture *(Southern Kyushu)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** JPY (cash recommended for ferries/local buses; cards accepted at major shops); Japanese (basic English at tourist hubs).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Visit Kagoshima (Official City Guide)](https://www.kagoshima-yokanavi.jp/en) | [Kagoshima Prefectural Tourism](https://www.kagoshima-kankou.com/for/)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships dock at **Marine Port Kagoshima**, roughly 6 km south of downtown Tenmonkan and the Sakurajima ferry terminal. Use the cruise shuttle bus, a 15–20 minute taxi, or walk 15 minutes to the Kagoshima City View / tram stop. Active volcano **Sakurajima** sits directly across Kinko Bay and occasionally dusts the city with light volcanic ash. **Confirm per call:** Marine Port shuttle drop-off, Sakurajima ferry schedule, and volcano activity advisories.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Sakurajima Active Volcano *(Ferry & Yunohira Observation Point)*** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 15–20 min taxi/shuttle to Kagoshima Port Ferry Terminal + 15 min 24-hour car/passenger ferry + island tour bus/taxi.<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (paved lava walkways, seaside footbath, and observation decks).<br>**Weather:** Moderate (exposed volcanic slopes; bring sunglasses/mask if light ash falls).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes (easy 3–3.5 hr round-trip). | Cross Kinko Bay by ferry to walk across dramatic 1914 lava fields, soak in a 100-metre volcanic hot-spring footbath, and view steaming smoking craters up close. | [Visit Kagoshima — Sakurajima](https://www.kagoshima-yokanavi.jp/en)<br>[Google Maps (31.5931, 130.6089)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=31.5931,130.6089) |
| **Sengan-en Garden & Shoko Shuseikan Museum *(UNESCO World Heritage)*** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 20–25 min taxi north along the bay (or Kagoshima City View sightseeing bus).<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (paved garden paths with gentle slopes and stone steps).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate (open-air coastal stroll garden; indoor Meiji industrial heritage museum).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | A 17th-century coastal feudal lord's villa and landscape garden that uses Kinko Bay and smoking Sakurajima volcano as its dramatic "borrowed scenery." | [Sengan-en Official](https://www.senganen.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (31.6181, 130.5789)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=31.6181,130.5789) |
| **Shiroyama Observatory & Tenmonkan Arcade** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 15–20 min taxi/shuttle to central Tenmonkan + 10 min bus/taxi up to Shiroyama.<br>**Physical:** Low (flat covered shopping arcades; paved hilltop viewing deck).<br>**Weather:** Low (covered Tenmonkan arcades; open viewpoint).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Combines Kagoshima's signature panoramic lookout over the city and Sakurajima with covered pedestrian arcades famous for Kagoshima *kurobuta* black pork and *shirokuma* shaved ice. | [Visit Kagoshima — Shiroyama](https://www.kagoshima-yokanavi.jp/en)<br>[Google Maps (31.5969, 130.5503)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=31.5969,130.5503) |
| **Ibusuki Natural Sand Baths *(Sunamushi)*** | Full-Day / Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 60–75 min drive south along Kagoshima Bay each way.<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (lying buried in warm volcanic sand for 10–15 min + indoor hot spring rinse).<br>**Weather:** None (covered beachside sand-bath pavilion).<br>**4-Hr Call:** No (allow 5–6 hours). | Experience a 300-year-old wellness ritual where attendants bury bathers up to the neck in naturally geothermally heated black volcanic beach sand. | [Ibusuki Tourism Official](https://www.ibusuki.or.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (31.2339, 130.6481)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=31.2339,130.6481) |

## 7. Kobe, Japan
* **Country / area:** Japan — Hyogo Prefecture *(Kansai Region; gateway to Osaka, Kyoto & Himeji)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** JPY (credit cards and IC cards widely accepted); Japanese (good English signage on rail networks and major sights).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Feel Kobe (Official City Guide)](https://www.feel-kobe.jp/en/) | [Port of Kobe — Cruise Information](https://www.city.kobe.lg.jp/a44800/kanko/leisure/harbor/passenger/)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships berth either at **Kobe Port Terminal** (Shinko Pier 4, directly connected to the Port Liner monorail to Sannomiya Station in 5 minutes, or a 15–20 minute flat walk) or **Naka Pier Cruise Terminal** right beside Kobe Port Tower and Meriken Park. **Confirm per call:** Kobe Port Terminal vs. Naka Pier berth, Port Liner connection, and Shinkansen/JR transfer times for Kyoto/Himeji.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Meriken Park, Kobe Harborland & Nankinmachi *(Chinatown)*** | Walk-Off / Independent | **Travel:** 5–15 min flat walk from Naka Pier (or 20–25 min walk / 10 min taxi from Kobe Port Terminal).<br>**Physical:** Low (flat waterfront promenades and pedestrian shopping streets).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate (covered Motomachi/Chinatown arcades; open waterfront).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Explores Kobe's landmark red steel Port Tower, waterfront promenade, and vibrant Chinatown alleyways famous for Kobe beef buns and dumplings. | [Feel Kobe — Waterfront](https://www.feel-kobe.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (34.6825, 135.1867)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=34.6825,135.1867) |
| **Kitano Ijinkan-gai *(19th-Century Foreign Residences)* & Ikuta Shrine** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 5 min Port Liner to Sannomiya + 15 min uphill walk (or 10 min taxi from pier).<br>**Physical:** Moderate (steep uphill streets in Kitano; stairs inside historic mansions).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate (indoor historic homes and shaded shrine grounds).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Wander steep hillside lanes lined with preserved Victorian and European consular mansions built after Kobe opened to foreign trade in 1868. | [Feel Kobe — Kitano](https://www.feel-kobe.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (34.7008, 135.1897)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=34.7008,135.1897) |
| **Nada Sake Brewery District *(Hakutsuru / Kikumasamune)*** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 15–20 min taxi east from port (or Hanshin Line train to Sumiyoshi/Uozaki + 10 min walk).<br>**Physical:** Low (flat residential streets and accessible brewery museums).<br>**Weather:** None (indoor wooden brewery museums and tasting rooms).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Japan's premier sake-producing region, where centuries-old breweries offer free historical exhibits and tastings of crisp, mineral-rich *nihonshu*. | [Hakutsuru Sake Brewery Museum](https://www.hakutsuru.co.jp/english/culture/museum.html)<br>[Google Maps (34.7169, 135.2669)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=34.7169,135.2669) |
| **Himeji Castle *(UNESCO World Heritage)* or Kyoto Cultural Highlights** | Full-Day / Guided Excursion or High-Speed Rail | **Travel:** 40 min JR Special Rapid train (or 60 min drive) west to Himeji; 50–70 min train/drive northeast to Kyoto.<br>**Physical:** Strenuous at Himeji (steep wooden ladders inside 6-storey original keep, shoes removed); Moderate in Kyoto.<br>**Weather:** Moderate.<br>**4-Hr Call:** No (allow 5–6 hours for Himeji; 7–9 hours for Kyoto). | Visits either Japan's grandest surviving original feudal castle ("White Heron Castle") or Kyoto's iconic Golden Pavilion, Fushimi Inari torii gates, and Gion district. | [Himeji Castle Official](https://www.city.himeji.lg.jp/castle/)<br>[Google Maps (34.8394, 134.6939)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=34.8394,134.6939) |

## 8. Kochi, Japan
* **Country / area:** Japan — Kochi Prefecture *(Southern Shikoku Island)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** JPY (cash strongly recommended for local markets, trams, and Katsurahama buses); Japanese (friendly volunteer greeters at pier; limited English in town).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Visit Kochi Japan (Official Prefectural Guide)](https://visitkochijapan.com/en/) | [Port of Kochi — Cruise Information](https://www.pref.kochi.lg.jp/soshiki/170301/)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships berth at **Kochi Shinko Pier** (roughly 8 km southeast of downtown Kochi). Courtesy port shuttles or local buses/taxis transfer passengers in 20–25 minutes to central Harimayabashi, Kochi Castle, and Hirome Market. **Confirm per call:** shuttle drop-off at Harimayabashi, Katsurahama MY-YU bus timetable, and Sunday Market day-of-week.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Kochi Castle & Hirome Market** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 20–25 min port shuttle/taxi to Harimayabashi + 10 min flat walk.<br>**Physical:** Moderate-Strenuous at Castle (steep stone steps and wooden keep ladders); Low at Hirome Market.<br>**Weather:** Low (indoor castle keep and covered food hall).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Tour one of Japan's twelve surviving original Edo-period castles (the only one retaining its complete palace residence) before feasting on flame-seared *katsuo no tataki* (bonito) at lively Hirome Market. | [Visit Kochi Japan — Kochi Castle](https://visitkochijapan.com/en/)<br>[Google Maps (33.5608, 133.5314)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=33.5608,133.5314) |
| **Makino Botanical Garden & Chikurin-ji Temple *(Mount Godaisan)*** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 15–20 min taxi/MY-YU tourist bus from cruise port or downtown.<br>**Physical:** Moderate (paved hillside garden loops, glass conservatory, and stone temple steps).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate (indoor architectural conservatory and shaded forest paths).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Set atop Mount Godaisan, this acclaimed botanical sanctuary showcases over 3,000 plant species alongside a five-storey pagoda on the historic Shikoku 88-Temple Pilgrimage. | [Kochi Prefectural Makino Botanical Garden](https://www.makino.or.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (33.5469, 133.5778)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=33.5469,133.5778) |
| **Katsurahama Beach & Sakamoto Ryoma Memorial Museum** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 15–20 min taxi/MY-YU bus southwest of cruise pier.<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (paved coastal pine promenade; steep slope/stairs to hilltop museum).<br>**Weather:** High (exposed Pacific coastline; strong surf—swimming prohibited).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | A scenic crescent pebble beach framed by pine trees and a towering bronze statue of samurai visionary Sakamoto Ryoma overlooking the Pacific Ocean. | [Visit Kochi Japan — Katsurahama](https://visitkochijapan.com/en/)<br>[Google Maps (33.4969, 133.5747)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=33.4969,133.5747) |
| **Niyodo River *(Niyodo Blue)* & Nakatsu Gorge** | Full-Day / Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 60–75 min drive west into Shikoku's mountains each way.<br>**Physical:** Moderate-Strenuous (rocky gorge walkways, bridges, and stone steps).<br>**Weather:** Moderate (mountain river valley).<br>**4-Hr Call:** No (allow 5.5–6.5 hours). | Journeys into Shikoku's rugged interior to witness crystal-clear cobalt-blue mountain waters rushing through dramatic rock gorges and waterfalls. | [Visit Kochi Japan — Niyodo River](https://visitkochijapan.com/en/)<br>[Google Maps (33.5681, 133.1531)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=33.5681,133.1531) |

## 9. Miyazaki, Japan
* **Country / area:** Japan — Miyazaki Prefecture *(Southeastern Kyushu; Aburatsu / Hososhima Ports)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** JPY (cash strongly recommended for local buses, shrines, and coastal cafes); Japanese (limited English outside major sights).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Visit Miyazaki (Official Prefectural Guide)](https://www.kanko-miyazaki.jp/english/) | [Miyazaki Port & Cruise Information](https://www.pref.miyazaki.lg.jp/)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships calling at "Miyazaki" typically berth either at **Port of Aburatsu** in Nichinan (roughly 50 km south of Miyazaki City, right beside Obi Castle Town and Udo Shrine) or **Port of Hososhima** in Hyuga (roughly 70 km north of Miyazaki City, closer to Takachiho Gorge). **Confirm per call:** Aburatsu vs. Hososhima port assignment, courtesy shuttle drop-off, and Nichinan coastal bus schedules.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Udo Jingu Cliffside Shrine & Sun Messe Nichinan *(Southern / Aburatsu Port)*** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 20–25 min taxi/coastal bus north from Aburatsu Port (or 60 min south from Miyazaki City).<br>**Physical:** Moderate (steep stone cliffside stairways down to the sea-cave shrine).<br>**Weather:** High (exposed Pacific coastal cliffs).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes (from Aburatsu); No (from Hososhima). | A vivid vermilion Shinto shrine tucked inside a coastal sea cave battered by Pacific waves, famous for tossing lucky clay pebbles (*undama*) at a turtle-shaped rock below. | [Visit Miyazaki — Udo Shrine](https://www.kanko-miyazaki.jp/english/)<br>[Google Maps (31.6503, 131.4669)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=31.6503,131.4669) |
| **Obi Castle Town *(Little Kyoto of Kyushu) (Southern / Aburatsu Port)*** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 15 min taxi or JR Nichinan Line train from Aburatsu Port.<br>**Physical:** Low (flat stone-walled samurai lanes and historic cedar mansions).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate.<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes (from Aburatsu). | Stroll serene moss-lined samurai streets, cedar forests, and preserved feudal residences with a local walking-map voucher redeemable for snacks and craft souvenirs. | [Visit Miyazaki — Obi Castle Town](https://www.kanko-miyazaki.jp/english/)<br>[Google Maps (31.6275, 131.3519)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=31.6275,131.3519) |
| **Aoshima Island Shrine & Devil's Washboard *(Oni no Sentakuita)*** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 45–50 min drive/train north from Aburatsu Port (or 25 min south from Miyazaki City).<br>**Physical:** Low (flat pedestrian bridge over corrugated tidal rock formations and subtropical palm shrine).<br>**Weather:** Moderate-High (exposed coastal island bridge).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Borderline (allow 4–4.5 hours from Aburatsu). | Crosses a bridge over surreal, parallel rows of eroded Miocene sandstone ("Devil's Washboard") to a tiny jungle-clad island shrine dedicated to marital harmony. | [Visit Miyazaki — Aoshima](https://www.kanko-miyazaki.jp/english/)<br>[Google Maps (31.8053, 131.4744)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=31.8053,131.4744) |
| **Takachiho Gorge *(Northern / Hososhima Port)*** | Full-Day / Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 75–90 min drive inland northwest from Hososhima Port (2.5+ hrs from Aburatsu).<br>**Physical:** Moderate-Strenuous (steep gorge stairs and riverside cliff trails; optional rowboat rental).<br>**Weather:** Moderate (steep volcanic basalt canyon).<br>**4-Hr Call:** No (strictly a 6–7.5 hour excursion from Hososhima). | A dramatic V-shaped volcanic basalt canyon where the 17-metre Manai Waterfall plunges into emerald-green river waters famed in Japanese creation mythology. | [Takachiho Town Tourism Official](https://takachiho-kanko.info/en/)<br>[Google Maps (32.7011, 131.3008)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=32.7011,131.3008) |

# Cruise Port Passenger Guide — Japan & East Asia Destination Content Dataset (Part 2)

> **Purpose:** Expanded destination-page content for 9 additional Japanese cruise ports in the cruise passenger app. Each port includes structured attraction descriptions, value propositions, practical logistics, verified visitor/tourism links, and coordinate-anchored Google Maps links ready for dynamic berth routing.

## Dynamic Routing Integration Note

To calculate real-time routes from the ship's confirmed pier or tender landing, replace the query links below with the Google Maps Directions URL template once the `SailingPortCall` record supplies `{berth_lat}` and `{berth_lng}`:

```text
https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin={berth_lat},{berth_lng}&destination={dest_lat},{dest_lng}
```

---

## 1. Muroran, Japan
* **Country / area:** Japan — Hokkaido *(Southern Hokkaido / Iburi Subprefecture)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** JPY (cash recommended for local buses, taxis, and seafood stalls; cards accepted at major venues); Japanese (limited English outside tourist centres).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Muroran Tourism Association *(Otasuke Net)*](https://muro-kanko.com/) | [Visit Hokkaido (Official Regional Guide)](https://www.visit-hokkaido.jp/en/) | [Port of Muroran — Cruise Information](https://www.city.muroran.lg.jp/main/org8400/cruise.html)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships berth at **Sakimori Wharf** beneath the dramatic Hakucho Bridge (a 15–20 minute walk or 5-minute shuttle/taxi to JR Muroran Station and downtown Chuo-cho) or at **Irie Wharf** closer to the city centre. Reaching Cape Chikyu or Lake Toya requires a taxi, local bus, or guided tour. **Confirm per call:** Sakimori vs. Irie berth, shuttle bus drop-off, and Noboribetsu/Lake Toya coach durations.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Cape Chikyu *(Earth Cape)* & Tokkarisho Lookout** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 15–20 min taxi from cruise pier (or local bus + 15 min uphill walk).<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (paved cliffside parking area and short stepped viewing terrace).<br>**Weather:** High (exposed 100m Pacific sea cliffs; brisk coastal winds).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Sheer 100-metre white volcanic cliffs jutting into the Pacific Ocean, offering an uninterrupted horizon where the curvature of the Earth is visibly apparent. | [Muroran Tourism — Cape Chikyu](https://muro-kanko.com/)<br>[Google Maps (42.3036, 141.0014)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=42.3036,141.0014) |
| **Hakucho Bridge & Muroran Aquarium / Michi-no-Eki Mitaro** | Walk-Off / Independent | **Travel:** 10–15 min flat waterfront walk from Sakimori Wharf.<br>**Physical:** Low (flat harbourside paths and roadside rest complex).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate.<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Explores Hokkaido's oldest aquarium alongside a lively harbourside market serving Muroran's famous yakitori (pork-and-onion skewers) and fresh scallops beneath eastern Japan's largest suspension bridge. | [Muroran Tourism — Hakucho Bridge](https://muro-kanko.com/)<br>[Google Maps (42.3458, 140.9553)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=42.3458,140.9553) |
| **Noboribetsu Jigokudani *(Hell Valley)* & Oyunuma Pond** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 35–40 min drive northeast via expressway (or 25 min JR train to Noboribetsu + 15 min bus).<br>**Physical:** Moderate (wooden boardwalks and paved volcanic valley paths with sulfur vents).<br>**Weather:** Moderate (steaming geothermal vents and mountain weather).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Borderline (allow 4–4.5 hours round-trip). | A dramatic steaming volcanic crater valley filled with boiling sulfurous hot springs, fumaroles, and mineral streams feeding Hokkaido's most famous onsen resort. | [Noboribetsu Tourism Official](https://noboribetsu-spa.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (42.4975, 141.1489)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=42.4975,141.1489) |
| **Lake Toya *(Toyako)* & Usuzan Ropeway *(Showa-Shinzan)*** | Full-Day / Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 45–55 min drive northwest each way.<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (step-free cable car gondola and paved crater observation decks).<br>**Weather:** High (volcanic summit winds and seasonal fog).<br>**4-Hr Call:** No (allow 5–6 hours). | Ascends active Mount Usu beside steaming red lava dome Showa-Shinzan for sweeping views over circular caldera Lake Toya and Nakajima Island. | [Lake Toya Onsen Tourism](https://www.laketoya.com/en/)<br>[Google Maps (42.5439, 140.8406)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=42.5439,140.8406) |

## 2. Nagasaki, Japan
* **Country / area:** Japan — Nagasaki Prefecture *(Western Kyushu)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** JPY (IC cards and credit cards accepted at major sights/trams; cash helpful for small eateries); Japanese (excellent English signage at Peace Park and Glover Garden).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Visit Nagasaki (Official City Guide)](https://travel.at-nagasaki.jp/en/) | [Port of Nagasaki — Cruise Terminal](https://www.pref.nagasaki.jp/)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships berth right in the heart of Nagasaki at **Matsugae International Cruise Terminal**, directly at the foot of Glover Garden and Oura Church (5-minute flat walk) and steps from the Oura-kaigan tram stop. Overflow vessels occasionally berth across the bay at **Kogakura Wharf** (15-minute shuttle/taxi to downtown). **Confirm per call:** Matsugae vs. Kogakura berth, Nagasaki Electric Tramway day pass, and Mount Inasa ropeway operating hours.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Glover Garden, Oura Church & Dutch Slope *(Oranda-zaka)*** | Walk-Off / Independent | **Travel:** 5–10 min walk directly from Matsugae Cruise Terminal.<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (outdoor covered escalators carry visitors to the top of hillside Glover Garden for a downhill stroll).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate (indoor historic Western residences and brick church).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Explores preserved 19th-century European merchant mansions overlooking Nagasaki Harbour alongside Japan's oldest surviving wooden Gothic-style Catholic church (1864). | [Glover Garden Official](https://glover-garden.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (32.7342, 129.8692)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=32.7342,129.8692) |
| **Nagasaki Peace Park, Hypocenter & Atomic Bomb Museum** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 20–25 min Nagasaki Streetcar (Line 1 to Matsuyamamachi) or 15 min taxi north of port.<br>**Physical:** Low (escalators from tram stop up to Peace Park; accessible museum galleries).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate (outdoor peace statues; indoor museum).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Commemorates the August 9, 1945 atomic bombing with the iconic Peace Statue, Hypocenter monolith, and deeply informative historical museum galleries. | [Nagasaki Atomic Bomb Museum Official](https://nabmuseum.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (32.7753, 129.8636)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=32.7753,129.8636) |
| **Dejima Historic Trading Post & Shinchi Chinatown** | Walk-Off / Independent | **Travel:** 15–20 min flat walk north from Matsugae terminal (or 5 min tram ride).<br>**Physical:** Low (flat reconstructed Edo-era island compound and pedestrian streets).<br>**Weather:** Low (indoor wooden merchant warehouses and covered Chinatown eateries).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Step inside the restored fan-shaped artificial island that served as Japan's sole window to Western trade via Dutch merchants during two centuries of national isolation. | [Dejima Official](https://nagasakidejima.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (32.7436, 129.8731)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=32.7436,129.8731) |
| **Mount Inasa Ropeway *(Inasayama Observatory)*** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 15 min taxi to Fuchi Shrine ropeway station + 5 min aerial gondola.<br>**Physical:** Low (step-free gondola and spiral glass observation deck).<br>**Weather:** High (333m summit exposed to harbour fog/wind).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Glides high above Nagasaki's steep amphitheatre hills for 360-degree panoramic views over the ship-filled harbour and surrounding islands. | [Nagasaki Ropeway Official](https://www.nagasaki-ropeway.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (32.7522, 129.8492)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=32.7522,129.8492) |

## 3. Nagoya, Japan
* **Country / area:** Japan — Aichi Prefecture *(Chubu Region)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** JPY (IC cards and credit cards widely accepted across rail/metro and major attractions); Japanese (good English signage on metro/museums).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Nagoya Info (Official City Guide)](https://www.nagoya-info.jp/en/) | [Port of Nagoya — Cruise Terminal](https://www.port-of-nagoya.jp/english/)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships dock at **Garden Pier Cruise Terminal** in Nagoya Port, directly beside the Port of Nagoya Public Aquarium and a 5-minute walk from Nagoyako Subway Station (Meiko Line, ~20–25 minutes into central Sakae or Nagoya Station). **Confirm per call:** Garden Pier berth, Meiko/Meijo subway transfer, and Toyota Commemorative Museum opening days (closed Mondays).

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Port of Nagoya Public Aquarium & SCMAGLEV and Railway Park** | Walk-Off / Independent or Short Rail | **Travel:** Aquarium is a 3 min flat walk from pier; SCMAGLEV Park is 15 min taxi (or Aonami Line train via Nagoya Station).<br>**Physical:** Low (fully accessible indoor facilities).<br>**Weather:** None (indoor aquarium and railway museum).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Pairs one of Japan's largest marine aquariums (featuring orcas and beluga whales) with JR Central's interactive museum of Shinkansen bullet trains and superconducting maglev technology. | [SCMAGLEV and Railway Park Official](https://museum.jr-central.co.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (35.0489, 136.8511)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.0489,136.8511) |
| **Nagoya Castle *(Honmaru Palace)* & Kinshachi Yokocho** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 25–30 min direct Meiko/Meijo Subway Line from Nagoyako to Nagoyajo Station + 5 min flat walk.<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (flat castle grounds; accessible single-storey reconstructed gilded palace).<br>**Weather:** Low (indoor opulent gold-leaf palace rooms; note main stone keep interior remains closed for timber reconstruction).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Showcases the dazzling, meticulously reconstructed Edo-period Honmaru Palace adorned with gold-leaf sliding screens, framed by castle walls topped with golden *shachihoko* tiger-carp ornaments. | [Nagoya Castle Official](https://www.nagoyajo.city.nagoya.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (35.1856, 136.8992)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.1856,136.8992) |
| **Toyota Commemorative Museum of Industry and Technology** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 25–30 min taxi from port (or subway to Nagoya Station + 1 stop on Meitetsu Line to Sako).<br>**Physical:** Low (vast, flat accessible red-brick exhibition halls).<br>**Weather:** None (entirely indoor museum).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Traces Toyota's fascinating evolution from automatic textile looms to modern robotics and automotive manufacturing inside historic brick factory halls. | [Toyota Commemorative Museum Official](https://www.tcmit.org/english/)<br>[Google Maps (35.1819, 136.8761)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.1819,136.8761) |
| **Atsuta Jingu Shrine & Osu Kannon Shopping District** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 15–20 min Meiko/Meijo Subway Line directly from Nagoyako Station.<br>**Physical:** Low (flat gravel shrine paths and covered pedestrian shopping arcades).<br>**Weather:** Low (shaded sacred camphor forest and covered Osu arcades).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Combines one of Shinto's most sacred forested shrines (guardian of the Imperial Sacred Sword) with vibrant covered shopping streets famous for Nagoya's miso-katsu and hitsumabushi eel. | [Atsuta Jingu Official](https://www.atsutajingu.or.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (35.1275, 136.9086)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.1275,136.9086) |

## 4. Okinawa (Naha), Japan
* **Country / area:** Japan — Okinawa Prefecture *(Ryukyu Islands)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** JPY (cards accepted at major shops/monorail; cash useful for Makishi Market and taxis); Japanese (English signage on Yui Rail and major sites).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Visit Okinawa Japan (Official Prefectural Guide)](https://visitokinawajapan.com/) | [Naha Port Authority — Cruise Terminals](https://www.nahaport.jp/)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships berth either at **Naha Cruise Terminal (Wakasa)** (a 15–20 minute flat walk or 5-minute taxi to Kokusai-dori) or at **Shinko Wharf (Tomari / Aja)** farther north (10–15 minute taxi/shuttle to downtown Naha). **Confirm per call:** Wakasa vs. Shinko Wharf berth, Yui Rail monorail station proximity, and Shuri Castle reconstruction status.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Kokusai-dori *(International Street)*, Makishi Public Market & Tsuboya Pottery District** | Walk-Off / Independent *(from Wakasa)* or Local Transit *(from Shinko)* | **Travel:** 15–20 min flat walk from Wakasa terminal (or 10 min taxi from Shinko).<br>**Physical:** Low (flat bustling boulevard, covered market alleys, and gentle stone lanes in Tsuboya).<br>**Weather:** Low (covered Heiwa-dori and Makishi food arcades).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Naha's vibrant main avenue and covered public market brimming with tropical dragonfruit, purple sweet-potato tarts, Okinawan soba, and traditional *yachimun* pottery workshops. | [Visit Okinawa — Naha](https://visitokinawajapan.com/)<br>[Google Maps (26.2144, 127.6881)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=26.2144,127.6881) |
| **Shurijo Castle Park & Tamaudun Royal Mausoleum *(UNESCO World Heritage)*** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 20 min Yui Rail monorail to Shuri Station + 15 min walk (or 15–20 min taxi from port).<br>**Physical:** Moderate (stone fortress gates, stairs, and hilltop park paths).<br>**Weather:** Moderate (open-air limestone citadel).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | The historic seat of the Ryukyu Kingdom, showcasing distinctive vermilion Okinawan-Chinese palace architecture (currently featuring live, transparent "reconstruction-in-progress" viewing halls). | [Shurijo Castle Park Official](https://oki-park.jp/shurijo/en/)<br>[Google Maps (26.2170, 127.7195)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=26.2170,127.7195) |
| **Okinawa World *(Gyokusendo Cave)* & Sefa-utaki Sacred Grove** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 30–35 min drive southeast of Naha.<br>**Physical:** Moderate-Strenuous (890m damp limestone cave walkway with stairs; steep sacred forest paths at Sefa-utaki).<br>**Weather:** Low inside cave (warm/humid 21°C); Moderate outdoors.<br>**4-Hr Call:** Borderline (allow 4–4.5 hours). | Explores a vast subterranean limestone cavern filled with thousands of stalactites alongside traditional Ryukyuan village crafts and Eisa taiko drum performances. | [Okinawa World Official](https://www.gyokusendo.co.jp/okinawaworld/en/)<br>[Google Maps (26.1394, 127.7503)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=26.1394,127.7503) |
| **Okinawa Churaumi Aquarium *(Ocean Expo Park)*** | Full-Day / Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 90–110 min drive north via Okinawa Expressway each way.<br>**Physical:** Low (fully accessible coastal aquarium and oceanfront park).<br>**Weather:** None (indoor aquarium halls).<br>**4-Hr Call:** No (strictly a 7–8.5 hour full-day excursion due to distance). | One of the world's premier aquariums, featuring the colossal Kuroshio Sea tank housing whale sharks and giant manta rays. | [Okinawa Churaumi Aquarium Official](https://churaumi.okinawa/en/)<br>[Google Maps (26.6942, 127.8781)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=26.6942,127.8781) |

## 5. Osaka, Japan
* **Country / area:** Japan — Osaka Prefecture *(Kansai Region; gateway to Kyoto & Nara)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** JPY (credit cards and IC cards widely accepted everywhere); Japanese (excellent English signage across metro/rail and major attractions).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Osaka Info (Official City Guide)](https://osaka-info.jp/en/) | [Port of Osaka — Tempozan Cruise Terminal](https://www.city.osaka.lg.jp/port/)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships berth at **Tempozan Passenger Terminal** in Osaka Bay, directly beside the Osaka Aquarium Kaiyukan and Tempozan Ferris Wheel, and a 5-minute flat walk from Osakako Subway Station (Chuo Line, ~15–20 minutes to central Namba/Dotonbori or Osaka Castle). **Confirm per call:** Tempozan berth, Chuo Line subway access, and Kyoto/Nara rail transfer times.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Osaka Aquarium Kaiyukan & Tempozan Harbor Village** | Walk-Off / Independent | **Travel:** 2–5 min flat walk directly from cruise terminal.<br>**Physical:** Low (accessible spiral descending walkway inside aquarium).<br>**Weather:** None (entirely indoor aquarium and shopping mall).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Steps from the ship, this world-renowned aquarium spirals around a massive Pacific Ocean tank featuring whale sharks, sea otters, and Ring of Fire marine habitats. | [Osaka Aquarium Kaiyukan Official](https://www.kaiyukan.com/language/eng/)<br>[Google Maps (34.6545, 135.4290)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=34.6545,135.4290) |
| **Dotonbori Canal, Shinsaibashi & Kuromon Ichiba Market** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 20 min Chuo Line subway (change at Hommachi to Midosuji Line to Namba) or 20 min taxi.<br>**Physical:** Low (flat neon-lit canal promenades and covered shopping arcades).<br>**Weather:** Low (miles of covered pedestrian arcades and indoor street-food stalls).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Osaka's electric culinary heart ("Japan's Kitchen"), famous for giant mechanical crab signs, Glico Running Man billboards, *takoyaki* octopus balls, and fresh market skewers. | [Osaka Info — Dotonbori](https://osaka-info.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (34.6687, 135.5013)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=34.6687,135.5013) |
| **Osaka Castle *(Osakajo)* & Nishinomaru Garden** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 20 min direct Chuo Line subway from Osakako to Tanimachi 4-chome Station + 15 min park walk.<br>**Physical:** Low-Moderate (wide paved park paths and stone moat bridges; elevator available inside museum keep).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate (indoor museum inside main tower; expansive park grounds).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Toyotomi Hideyoshi's monumental 16th-century fortress surrounded by colossal granite moat walls and a museum detailing Japan's feudal unification. | [Osaka Castle Official](https://www.osakacastle.net/)<br>[Google Maps (34.6873, 135.5259)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=34.6873,135.5259) |
| **Kyoto *(Kinkaku-ji, Fushimi Inari & Gion)* or Nara Deer Park & Todai-ji** | Full-Day / Guided Excursion or Rail | **Travel:** 55–75 min train/drive northeast to Kyoto; 50–65 min train/drive east to Nara.<br>**Physical:** Moderate (temple stairs, gravel courtyards, and torii-gate paths).<br>**Weather:** Moderate.<br>**4-Hr Call:** No (strictly a 6.5–8.5 hour full-day excursion). | Easy access to either Japan's ancient imperial capital of Kyoto or Nara's giant bronze Great Buddha hall surrounded by hundreds of bowing sacred deer. | [Kyoto City Official Travel Guide](https://kyoto.travel/en/)<br>[Google Maps (35.0394, 135.7292)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.0394,135.7292) |

## 6. Sakaiminato, Japan
* **Country / area:** Japan — Tottori Prefecture *(San'in / Sea of Japan Coast; gateway to Matsue & Izumo)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** JPY (cash strongly recommended for local buses, markets, and small restaurants; cards accepted at major museums); Japanese (courtesy port shuttles and helpful volunteers at pier).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Sakaiminato Tourism Guide](https://www.sakaiminato.net/foreign/en/) | [Visit Tottori (Official Prefectural Guide)](https://www.tottori-tour.jp/en/)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships dock at **Sakai-Yumeminato Terminal** (roughly 4 km south of JR Sakaiminato Station and Mizuki Shigeru Road) or at **Showa-cho Wharf** closer to town. Courtesy port shuttles typically connect the terminal to JR Sakaiminato Station (10 minutes), where trains and buses link to Matsue and Adachi Museum. **Confirm per call:** Yumeminato vs. Showa-cho berth, shuttle drop-off at Sakaiminato Station, and Adachi Museum shuttle bus times.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Mizuki Shigeru Road *(Yokai Street)* & Sakaiminato Fish Market** | Local Transit / Independent | **Travel:** 10 min courtesy shuttle/taxi from Yumeminato terminal to JR Sakaiminato Station.<br>**Physical:** Low (flat 800m sidewalk lined with over 170 bronze folklore monster statues).<br>**Weather:** Low (covered storefronts, Yokai Museum, and indoor seafood centre).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Celebrates native manga creator Shigeru Mizuki with 177 whimsical bronze *yokai* spirit statues lining the street from the station to a bustling Sea of Japan snow-crab market. | [Sakaiminato Tourism — Mizuki Road](https://www.sakaiminato.net/foreign/en/)<br>[Google Maps (35.5453, 133.2256)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.5453,133.2256) |
| **Adachi Museum of Art *(Yasugi)*** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 35–40 min drive southwest (or JR train to Yasugi Station + 20 min free museum shuttle bus).<br>**Physical:** Low (fully accessible indoor viewing galleries looking out onto the gardens).<br>**Weather:** None (gardens are admired through panoramic glass windows year-round).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Borderline (allow 4–4.5 hours round-trip). | Repeatedly ranked Japan's #1 Japanese garden for over 20 consecutive years, framed like living landscape paintings alongside masterworks by Yokoyama Taikan. | [Adachi Museum of Art Official](https://www.adachi-museum.or.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (35.3769, 133.1944)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.3769,133.1944) |
| **Matsue Castle *(National Treasure)* & Horikawa Moat Boat Cruise** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 40–45 min drive/bus west across Lake Nakaumi.<br>**Physical:** Moderate-Strenuous (steep wooden interior stairs inside original 1611 castle keep; seated covered moat boats).<br>**Weather:** Low-Moderate (covered wooden canal boats with low bridges).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Borderline (allow 4.5 hours round-trip). | Tour one of Japan's twelve surviving original wooden castle keeps before gliding through historic samurai-district moats on a traditional thatched-roof canal boat. | [Visit Matsue Official](https://www.visit-matsue.com/)<br>[Google Maps (35.4750, 133.0506)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.4750,133.0506) |
| **Izumo Taisha Grand Shrine *(Izumo-oyashiro)*** | Full-Day / Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 75–90 min drive west each way.<br>**Physical:** Low (wide flat pine-lined sacred approaches and gravel courtyards).<br>**Weather:** Moderate (open-air shrine precinct).<br>**4-Hr Call:** No (allow 5.5–6.5 hours). | One of Japan's oldest and most revered Shinto shrines, famous for its massive twisted sacred straw rope (*shimenawa*) and association with marriage and good fortune. | [Visit Shimane — Izumo Taisha](https://www.kankou-shimane.com/en/)<br>[Google Maps (35.4019, 132.6856)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.4019,132.6856) |

## 7. Shimizu (Mount Fuji), Japan
* **Country / area:** Japan — Shizuoka Prefecture *(Suruga Bay; gateway to Mount Fuji & Hakone)*.
* **Currency / language baseline:** JPY (cash recommended for local buses, water taxis, and fish market stalls; cards accepted at major shops); Japanese (friendly port volunteers; Mount Fuji visibility is notoriously weather-dependent).
* **Official Tourism & Port Links:** [Visit Shizuoka (Official Prefectural Guide)](https://exploreshizuoka.jp/) | [Port of Shimizu — Cruise Information](https://www.portofshimizu.com/)
* **Getting ashore:** Cruise ships berth at **Hinode Wharf**, right beside the S-Pulse Dream Plaza waterfront complex (a 5-minute flat walk) and water-bus pier to Miho no Matsubara. Courtesy port shuttles connect Hinode Wharf to JR Shimizu Station (10 minutes). **Confirm per call:** Hinode Wharf berth, shuttle to JR Shimizu Station, and live Mount Fuji cloud visibility.

| Attraction | Access Tier | Practical Logistics | Why It Is Worth Visiting | Research & Routing Links |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Miho no Matsubara Pine Grove *(UNESCO World Heritage)*** | Walk-Off / Independent *(via Water Bus)* | **Travel:** 15 min Shimizu Port Water Bus directly from Hinode Wharf (beside ship) + 15 min flat walk (or 15 min taxi).<br>**Physical:** Low (flat paved seaside pine walkway; dark volcanic sand beach).<br>**Weather:** High (clear skies required to see Mount Fuji rising across Suruga Bay).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | A 7-kilometre shoreline lined with 30,000 ancient twisted pine trees immortalized in woodblock prints for framing Mount Fuji across Suruga Bay. | [Visit Shizuoka — Miho no Matsubara](https://exploreshizuoka.jp/)<br>[Google Maps (34.9942, 138.5236)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=34.9942,138.5236) |
| **Kashi-no-Ichi Fish Market & S-Pulse Dream Plaza *(Sushi Museum)*** | Walk-Off / Independent | **Travel:** 5 min flat walk from Hinode Wharf to Dream Plaza; 15 min walk (or 5 min water bus/shuttle) to Kashi-no-Ichi.<br>**Physical:** Low (flat harbourfront boardwalks and indoor market halls).<br>**Weather:** None (indoor tuna market and waterfront mall).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes. | Shimizu lands more frozen tuna than anywhere else in Japan—sample ultra-fresh *maguro* bowls at Kashi-no-Ichi Market and explore Japan's first Sushi Museum. | [Kashi-no-Ichi Market Official](https://kashinoichi.com/)<br>[Google Maps (35.0219, 138.4919)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.0219,138.4919) |
| **Nihondaira Yume Terrace & Kunozan Toshogu Shrine** | Local Transit / Independent or Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 25–30 min taxi/bus up Nihondaira plateau + 5 min Nihondaira Ropeway down to shrine.<br>**Physical:** Low at Yume Terrace (accessible timber observation deck); Strenuous at Kunozan Toshogu (steep stone stairways between shrine pavilions).<br>**Weather:** High (307m hilltop plateau; clouds can hide Fuji).<br>**4-Hr Call:** Yes (3–3.5 hr round-trip). | Pairs Kengo Kuma's architectural hilltop terrace overlooking Suruga Bay and tea plantations with the ornate, gold-lacquered mausoleum shrine of shogun Tokugawa Ieyasu. | [Nihondaira Yume Terrace Official](https://nihondaira-yume-terrace.jp/en/)<br>[Google Maps (34.9711, 138.4658)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=34.9711,138.4658) |
| **Mount Fuji 5th Station, Shiraito Falls & Fuji Five Lakes *(Kawaguchiko)*** | Full-Day / Guided Excursion | **Travel:** 75–110 min drive north each way depending on destination.<br>**Physical:** Moderate (altitude at 2,300m 5th Station; stepped paved path to Shiraito Falls).<br>**Weather:** High (mountain cloud cover and cool temperatures even in summer).<br>**4-Hr Call:** No (strictly a 6–8 hour full-day excursion). | Journeys right to the foothills and mid-slope of Japan's iconic 3,776-metre sacred peak, visiting spring-fed Shiraito Falls and lakeside viewpoints. | [Visit Shizuoka — Mount Fuji](https://exploreshizuoka.jp/)<br>[Google Maps (35.3125, 138.5872)](https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=35.3125,138.5872) |
